## 2026-11-22: replays check what they type, and saved files hold up (#41, after #42)

giulioc84's benchmark (#41) found a replay that saved `+10655555552` for `0655555552` and said `replayed 6 steps`; #42 (merged) fixed the phone rule and made a `verified: false` stop the replay. This branch does the rest of the issue's bugs and the cheap half of its closed-loop proposal.

- `Replay::act` (`commands.rs`): a typed step whose text is not confirmed stops, including a verdict nothing can give; a saved `type --submit` is sent without Enter, and `press enter` follows once the field shows the text. First line: `replayed 1 step in 0.4 s, 1 typed text verified`; `--json` has `verified`.
- `same_phone_number` (`verify.rs`) is the typed-field rule as one function; `Session::seek` uses it as a last resort for a search text of six or more digits, so `wait "0655555552"` finds `065-555-5552` and `(065) 555 5552`, and not `+1 065-555-5552`.
- `show_saved` names each typed value no later `wait` reads back.
- `lasting()` (`journal.rs`) names a ref the last screen dropped by the label of the field as last seen (bug 3). `Actor::act` keeps a failed `scroll --until` that moved the list as the plain scrolls that moved it (bug 4), and `Kept` drops `changed` after a failed action.
- `{{name:example}}` blanks (`variables.rs`): the "needs" message shows `day1 (like "Tuesday")`.
- Docs: D60 and D98 amended, `docs/reference.md` saving and variables, the site's replay-in-CI page, CHANGELOG Unreleased.

Tests, Mac mini, `cargo test --locked -p phonedriver` with the 0.1.6 agent jar (no device):
- First runs: `a_replayed_type_and_submit_whose_text_did_not_land_sends_no_enter` failed with `left: 1 right: 0` ("Enter went out with the wrong text"); `..._landed_sends_enter_once_and_says_verified` failed on the missing `pressed enter`; `a_wait_for_a_typed_phone_number_finds_it_formatted_but_not_behind_a_country_code` panicked at its first lookup (`065-555-5552` not found); `a_blank_carries_an_example...` did not compile against the old `fill` (its error type had no examples). The two tests #42 parked: with only the scroll kept, `a_failed_scroll_that_moved_the_list_is_kept_for_the_step_after_it` still failed at the tap (`it changed nothing this time; when it was saved it changed the screen`), because the tap's `changed` had been measured against the screen before the failed scroll; dropping `changed` after a failure fixed it. `save_names_each_typed_value_no_later_wait_reads_back` was written after its code, not before.
- After: lib `161 passed` (162 with the blanks test), `cli 33`, `daemon 210`, `mcp 55`, `replay 11 passed; 0 ignored`. `cargo clippy --locked -p phonedriver --all-targets -- -D warnings`: clean. `cargo fmt --all`: applied.

Not run on a device: the submit split costs one more settle per saved `type --submit` (`TYPED_QUIET`, 150 ms, on a fake device); no emulator replay of the contact flow yet.
## 2026-11-22: issue #43, a relabeled text keeps its ref, the audit says what a size is measured from, doctor names the UiAutomation hold

- **A text that shrinks to its new label keeps its ref** (`crates/core/src/refs.rs`). Cause: the identity match (same window and `sourceNodeId`) keeps an element whose label changed only if its center moved under 8 points. A React Native text is as wide as its words, so "Language" to "Lingua" shrank it from its right edge and moved its center about 10 points: the diff said `- @e1` and `+ @e16 text "Lingua"`, while full-width or centered texts kept their refs and came out as `~`. The first guess, a plain `ScrollView` taken for a recycling list, is wrong: `ScrollView` is `Role::Other`, so the list rule never applied. Now the center or any corner staying within 8 points counts as the same place; inside a list the recycled-row rule is unchanged. Test first (win-pc WSL, `cargo test --locked -p phonedriver-core -p phonedriver --lib refs::`): `a_text_that_shrinks_to_its_new_label_keeps_its_ref` failed with `left: ["e1", "e3"]`, `right: ["e1", "e2"]`; after, all 17 `refs::` tests pass (and the whole `phonedriver-core` lib, `242 passed`), including `a_recycled_list_slot_showing_another_item_in_the_same_place_is_a_new_element` and the proptest `refs_are_stable_unique_and_never_reused`. `docs/architecture.md` section 4.3 rule 1 updated.
- **The audit says what a size is measured from** (`crates/core/src/audit.rs`, `actor.rs` `audited`). `audit` now returns `Finding::Unnamed` or `Finding::SmallTarget`; when any small-target finding is listed, the audit ends with one line: `sizes are the bounds accessibility reports; a touch area the app enlarges (React Native `hitSlop`) is not seen`. It is said for every app, not only React Native ones: the agent reports `getClassName()`, and a React Native view's is `android.view.ViewGroup` or `android.widget.TextView`, so the tree cannot tell a React Native app from another. No line is added when there is no small-target finding, or without `--audit`. Test first: `cargo test --locked -p phonedriver --test cli small_target`: `a_small_target_finding_says_its_size_is_the_reported_bounds` failed at `cli.rs:656` (the output ended at `audit: 1 finding`, no note); after, the output ends `@e1 button "Language": 169x40pt, under 44x44pt` then the note, and the test passes with `snapshot_audit_adds_the_screens_accessibility_problems`. D90's Limits updated.
- **doctor names the UiAutomation hold** (`crates/cli/src/doctor.rs`). The checks of an agent that answered are built by `answered`, and its `uiautomation` line now reads `connected, the screen tree reads; while phonedriver holds it, `uiautomator dump` and other UiAutomation clients are killed (`phonedriver daemon stop` frees it)`. doctor's `release` only drops the lease, so the agent stays connected after doctor and the line holds. `docs/reference.md` doctor paragraph has it; the site's troubleshooting page already did. Test first: `doctor::tests::a_healthy_agent_says_it_holds_uiautomation_and_how_to_free_it` failed with the old line (`panicked at crates/cli/src/doctor.rs:1126:9`), passes with the new one.
- Checks (win-pc WSL, Rust 1.99.1): `cargo fmt --all --check` clean; `cargo clippy --locked --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings` `Finished`; `cargo test --locked --no-fail-fast -p phonedriver-core -p phonedriver --lib`: `160 passed` and `242 passed`; `--test cli --test daemon --test mcp`: `36`, `210`, `55 passed`. Not run: a React Native app on a device (none here), so the #43 shape is reproduced from the report's description, not from its tree.
## 2026-11-22: a saved run keeps each launched app's build, and a replay refuses another (D109, #41 proposal 4)

- `Device::app_build` (default `None`): Android one `dumpsys package <pkg> | grep -E 'versionCode=|versionName='` → `4.37.1 (437001)` (first of each is the installed package); Simulator `simctl appinfo` → `CFBundleShortVersionString (CFBundleVersion)`; physical iPhone `None`. Daemon method `build {app}`. The `journal` call adds `build` to each launch step, one read per app, so only `save` pays it.
- `Replay::play` reads each launched app's build once before step 1 and refuses on a mismatch naming both, hint `--any-build` (MCP `any_build`). The GitHub Action passes `--any-build`: it installs the build under test.
- Test first (Mac mini, `cargo test -p phonedriver --test replay -- build`): with the check switched off, `a_replay_refuses_another_build_of_an_app_it_launches_unless_told_any_build` panicked with `replayed 1 step in 0.7 s / 1. launched Example` (it ran on build 5.0 (500) a file saved on 4.37.1 (437001)); `save_keeps_the_build_of_each_app_it_launches` did not compile before `FakeDevice::set_app_build` and `build` existed. After: `ok. 2 passed`. Parsers: `the_version_is_read_from_the_package_dump_and_the_installed_one_comes_first` (android lib), `appinfo_gives_the_version_and_the_build` (ios lib), both ok.
- `cargo clippy --locked --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings`: clean. `cargo test -p phonedriver --test cli --test mcp --test daemon --test replay --lib`: 33, 55 (tool-list budget test included), 210, 13, 161 passed.
- Not run on a device: the `dumpsys` grep and `simctl appinfo` were checked as parsers on recorded text only.

## 2026-11-22: a replayed step checks it is on the kind of screen it was saved on (D110, #41 proposal 5)

- Saved act steps hold `"on": {"app", "over"}` from the session's last screen (no read). `over` = the front app window is narrower than the screen (Android dialog windows: the three dialog fixtures start 28 px in, every full-screen fixture at 0), a header alert, or a Dialog/Alert element. New `Header::popup`, set in the actor's render, serialized only when true, so replies and snapshots are unchanged.
- A replay sends `on`; `Actor::on_screen` refuses before any touch on another app or when `over` differs, reading the screen only when the session has none it is sure of. Launch, open, back, home and an action after a failed one keep no `on`.
- Test first (Mac mini): with `on_screen` switched off, `a_replayed_step_stops_before_touching_a_dialog_that_was_not_in_front_when_it_was_saved` and `a_replayed_step_saved_in_another_app_stops_before_touching_this_one` both panicked with `replayed 1 step in 0.5 s` (the tap went out). With it: `replay` 16 passed. `the_journal_holds_each_action_that_worked_…` now expects `"on": {"app": "com.example", "over": false}` in its step.
- `cargo fmt --all --check` clean; `cargo clippy --locked --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings` clean; `cargo test -p phonedriver-core -p phonedriver --lib --test ios_fixture --test replay --test daemon --test cli --test mcp`: 161, 33, 210, 55, 16, core 241 (snapshots unchanged), 5 passed. Also `guard` 54.
- Not run on a device: the window-width rule rests on the recorded fixtures, not a live dialog.

## 2026-11-22: audit fixes (branch fix/audit, MacBook, `cargo` with `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=6`; blaze stops on "layout of Coroutine … Unknown" in `FakeDevice::text`)

Baseline before any change: `cargo test --locked --workspace --no-fail-fast`, every suite `ok` (lib 163, daemon 210, mcp 55, core 242).

- **A call's reply is matched by id** (`client.rs` `read_reply`). The MCP server keeps one daemon connection; a tool call the client cancelled after its request went out left its reply on the socket, and the next call took it as its own. Replies with an earlier id are skipped. A daemon that drops the connection now says, in `hint`, which `daemon.log` holds why. Test first: `a_reply_left_by_a_cancelled_call_is_not_taken_for_the_next_calls` and `a_daemon_that_goes_away_mid_call_says_where_its_log_is` did not compile against the old inline loop (which broke on the first non-progress line, the id-3 reply); after, `cargo test -p phonedriver --lib client::` `6 passed`.
- **A mismatched client no longer replaces a daemon someone else is using** (`daemon/mod.rs` `greeted`, `client.rs`). Each connection whose `hello` succeeded is counted; the version-mismatch reply carries `others` and, when it is above 0, a hint naming `phonedriver daemon stop` instead of "the CLI restarts the daemon". The client replaces the daemon only when `others` is 0 or missing (an older daemon). Architecture §5 Version updated. Test first: `a_client_of_another_version_is_told_when_the_daemon_serves_someone_else` panicked at `daemon.rs:456` (`others` was `null`); after, `1 passed`.
- **The reads after a settle share one deadline** (`actor.rs` `REPORT_WAIT`, 10 s, the longest single wait). `report` ran stabilize, the lost-lines, empty and bare waits, the web-text and search waits, and stabilize again, each with its own limit. Test first (`--test daemon the_reads_after_a_settle_stop_at_one_deadline_on_a_page_that_never_loads`, paused clock, a spinner for 60 reads then a childless web view): `the reply took 16.05s`, over the 11 s bound; after, `212 passed` for the whole daemon suite. Architecture §4.2 header paragraph says so.
- **The guard judges a ref tap on the screen it was resolved on** (`actor.rs` `fresh` keeps its read as `guard_view`; `guard_tap` looks the element up there instead of in `s.last`). Test first (`--test guard a_ref_whose_button_now_says_pay_is_judged_by_what_it_says_now`: snapshot shows `@e1 button "Add to list"`, the screen relabels it `Pay $20.00`, tap `e1`): panicked at `guard.rs:36` "expected a refusal, got Ok", the tap was sent. After: refused with `label: "Pay $20.00"`, no gesture; `guard 55`, `guard_property 7`, `daemon 212`, `mcp 55` passed, and `point_taps_in_a_row_read_the_screen_once…` still counts 1 read, so a tap pays no extra read. D27 update 2026-11-22 (1).
- **An approval needs a choice the prompt offered, the prompt preselects nothing, and screen text in it is one line each** (`mcp.rs` `decision`, `schema`, `commitment`, `one_line`; the no-answer texts). Test first (`--lib mcp::tests`): `only_a_named_choice_is_an_approval` panicked at `mcp.rs:1999` (an accept with no choice was `Once`), `the_approval_prompt_has_no_answer_chosen_in_advance` at `:2006` (`"default":"allow"`), `screen_text_in_the_approval_prompt_cannot_add_lines_of_its_own` at `:2023` (the label's line breaks made 4 lines). After: `9 passed`; `--test guard` 55 and `--test mcp` 55 passed. D27 update 2026-11-22 (2) to (5).
- **`autonomous` holds for `batch` and `then` chains; one dispatcher for both MCP protocols** (`mcp.rs` `Touch`, `touch()`; `BatchArgs.autonomous`; `chain` and `guarded_batch` take it; `batch_steps` refuses a step that carries it). The `#[tool]` methods (connected clients) and `call_guarded` (protocol 2026-07-28) had separate copies of the same six branches; only the first turned an empty `type` into a clear. Test first (`--test guard`, both protocols): `autonomous_holds_for_a_batch_and_a_then_chain` panicked at `guard.rs:356` on 2026-07-28 (`unknown field autonomous`) and at `:598` on the older protocol (the chain asked); `mrtr::empty_text_clears_the_field_on_either_protocol` at `:630` (no `SetValue("")`). After: `guard 59`, `mcp 55`, lib `168` passed. `guard_property`'s model of an answer now says a bare accept licenses nothing (it said it did); with that, `7 passed`. D27 update (6), (7); architecture §8 guard paragraph.
- **The guard folds a label before it classifies it** (new `phonedriver_core::text::fold`, dependency `unicode-normalization 0.1.26`; `guard::tokens` and `classify`). Test first: `guard::tests::a_label_in_another_code_point_form_is_the_same_label` failed on the first case, `"Ｐａｙ ｎｏｗ"` → `None`. After: `guard::` 13 passed, `text::the_same_words_in_other_code_points_fold_alike` passed, `--test guard` and `--test guard_property` passed. D27 update (8).
- **`cargo test -p phonedriver-core` builds on its own** (`crates/core/Cargo.toml` dev tokio gains `time`). `testing.rs` sleeps with `tokio::time`, which only the workspace's feature unification turned on: alone it stopped with `cannot find time in tokio` at `testing.rs:461`. After: `243 passed`, `5 passed`.
- **On a physical device, `shell input`, installs and CALL intents are refused, and `open` takes an `intent:` link only with VIEW or MAIN** (`shell.rs` `refuse_on_physical`, `intent_action`; `actor.rs` Open). Test first: `shell::tests::touches_installs_and_calls_the_guard_cannot_see_are_refused_on_a_physical_device` panicked on `input tap 540 1650` (passed); `--test daemon an_intent_link_that_does_more_than_view_is_refused_on_a_physical_phone` panicked at `daemon.rs:232` ("expected an error", the summary read `opened intent:5551234#Intent;scheme=tel;action=android.intent.action.CALL;end`), and again with only the actor change backed out. After: lib 170, daemon 213, cli 34 passed. D21 safety list and D27 update (9).
- **`find -delete` and `find -exec` are writes, refused from the storage root on a physical device** (`shell.rs` `FIND_ACTS`). `only_reads` called every `find` read-only (no settle), and `find /sdcard -delete` passed the physical check that `rm -rf /sdcard` fails. Test first: the shell test panicked on `find /sdcard -delete`; after, `shell::tests` 8 passed, and `find /sdcard/Download -name '*.tmp' -delete` still runs. D21 list.
- **An empty Simulator shell command is refused, not a panic** (`ios/simulator.rs` `shell_args`). `shell ""`, `"   "` or `"''"` reached `mac_program`, whose `words[1..]` panicked the device actor. Test first: `an_empty_command_is_refused_instead_of_panicking` panicked at `simulator.rs:260` "range start index 1 out of range for slice of length 0"; after, `simulator::` 19 passed.
- **MCP `replay` reads only a `.steps` file inside its folder** (`mcp.rs` `replayable`, beside `savable`). It joined any `file` the model gave, so `/etc/passwd` or `../x.steps` was read and its parse errors could quote it. Test first: `mcp::tests::a_replay_reads_only_a_steps_file_inside_its_folder` did not compile (no check existed); after, `10 passed`. `--test mcp` `a_shell_tool_call_and_a_replayed_shell_step…` replayed a `steps.jsonl` in its folder and now names it `shell.steps`; `mcp 55`, `replay 16` passed. Reference MCP replay paragraph.
- **A PIN or card security code field takes only a secret** (`secret_field.rs` `names_a_card_code`; `SecretKind::CardCode` in core). Test first: `--test daemon plain_text_is_refused_in_a_pin_or_card_security_code_field` panicked at `daemon.rs:232`, `"summary": "typed into @e1"` for `CVV` with plain `4931`. After: the `plain_text` tests 7 passed (the look-alikes `Pin location`, `Search pins`, `Spinner` still take plain text); lib 171, daemon 214, cli 34, secret_property 3 passed. D30's secret-field Limits amended; both SKILL.md copies say so (still byte-identical).
- **MCP argument errors quote no value** (`wire::without_value`; `mcp.rs` `without_values` on rmcp's results, `Touch::parse` and `step_verb` errors). Test first (`--test guard a_malformed_argument_is_named_without_its_value`, both protocols): on 2026-07-28 `Mcp error: -32602: invalid type: string "hunter2pass", expected a boolean`, on the older protocol `failed to deserialize parameters: invalid type: string "hunter2pass"…`. After: `2 passed`; guard 61, mcp 55, lib 171 passed. D70 update 2026-11-22.
- **A held question keeps a hash of its call, not the call** (`mcp.rs` `held_key`: SipHash with a per-process key over the tool and arguments). The 2026-07-28 path kept `json!([tool, args]).to_string()` while a question was open, a `type`'s secret text included. Test first: `a_held_call_is_known_by_a_hash_that_holds_none_of_its_text` did not compile before `held_key`; after, `11 passed`; `guard 61` and `guard_property 7` (stale and mismatched retries still never tap) passed.
- **A secret is not sent to an iPhone over plain Wi-Fi from a Mac** (`ios/device.rs` `in_clear`, checked first in `text`). `Host::Network` is TCP to the runner's port with nothing encrypting it, so a `PasteSecret` crossed the LAN as written. Test first: `a_secret_is_not_sent_over_the_runner_port_nothing_encrypts` did not compile before `in_clear`; after, `device::` 4 passed. Not run on an iPhone (none on this machine). D106 update.
- **`wait` replies with what changed, as an action does** (`actor.rs` `answer`, used by `wait_for` and `wait_settled`). Both answered with `Kind::Full`, the whole screen, after the agent had just seen it. Test first (`--test daemon a_wait_answers_with_what_changed_since_the_screen_the_agent_saw`): panicked at `daemon.rs:1155`, `"kind":"full"` with the whole `@e1 button "One"` screen. After: an unchanged screen is an empty diff with `changed: false`, a new button is `+ @e2 button "Two"` alone, a session that saw nothing still gets the screen; daemon 215, cli 34, mcp 55, replay 16, dx 6, lib 172 passed. Architecture §4 waits paragraph.
- **The MCP instructions stop asking for a snapshot after `launch`** (`mcp.rs` `instructions`): `launch` returns the screen it opens, and "then `snapshot` once" made every task spend a call showing it again. Now: "`launch` … returns the screen; act on its @refs … (`snapshot` only to see the whole screen again)". Test `the_server_does_not_ask_for_a_snapshot_after_launch` asserts the old phrase is gone (it was in the text); `mcp 56` passed, the tool-list budget test included.
- **"The tap may have missed" follows only a tap** (`commands.rs` `show_action`). Any settled action that changed nothing got it: a swipe, a key, and now a `wait settled` whose diff is empty. A tap or long press keeps it; another action gets `nothing changed on screen: … screenshot shows the picture`; `screen settled` gets no hint. Test first: `only_a_tap_that_changed_nothing_is_told_it_may_have_missed` panicked at `commands.rs:3149` (`screen settled` carried the tap hint); after, lib 173, cli 34, dx 6, mcp 56 passed.
- **`phonedriver mcp` leaves without starting a daemon** (`mcp.rs` `leave`). On exit it sent `release` through `run`, which connects and, with no daemon running (a client that only listed tools, or a daemon that idled out), spawned one to release nothing. It now releases over the connection it holds, if any; the daemon also forgets an MCP client's sessions when that connection closes. Test `a_server_that_never_connected_leaves_without_connecting` (new `leave`); mcp lib 12, `--test mcp` 56 passed.
- **A failed Android OS or serial read is asked again after 2 s** (`daemon/android.rs` `Reads`, `RETRY_FAILED`). Each read's `.ok()` was kept per connection, so one failure (a phone still booting) left it listed with no version, and USB plus Wi-Fi of the same phone listed twice, until it reconnected. Test first (`--test adb_source a_failed_serial_read_while_listing_is_asked_again_on_the_next_list`): `the failed read was kept: 1 then 1`. Retrying on every list broke `once_tracking_listing_devices_does_not_ask_the_adb_server_again` (14 requests against 4: its fake never answers the version), so a failure is kept for 2 s; after, `adb_source 4`, `device_list 16`, `device_choice 7`, `sources` passed.
- **An iPhone that cannot be described no longer empties the device list** (`daemon/ios.rs` `described_only`, `each_found`). `found` ended with `?` on the first `describe` error, so one locked or untrusted iPad failed `devices` for every device, the Android ones included. Now it is left out while others list, its error is the answer only when it is the only device, and `connect` to it returns its own error. The lock across `describe` stays: it is what keeps two lists from opening two CoreDevice tunnels. Test first: `a_device_that_cannot_be_described_leaves_the_others_listed` did not compile before `described_only`; after, `daemon::ios` 3 passed, clippy clean. Not run on an iPhone.
- **A shell packet's length is capped before its buffer is made** (`android/adb.rs` `MAX_SHELL_PACKET`, adb's 1 MiB `MAX_PAYLOAD`). `ShellStream::next` allocated whatever 32-bit length the wire gave. Test first: `a_shell_packet_that_claims_gigabytes_is_an_error_not_an_allocation` (a frame claiming 0xffffffff bytes) failed with `shell stream read failed: Invalid argument (os error 22)`, after the 4 GiB buffer was made; after, it is the packet error, and the android lib's 92 tests passed.
- **`daemon.log` is moved to `daemon.log.1` past 4 MiB before a new daemon writes** (`client.rs` `rotate`, `LOG_LIMIT`). Every daemon appended to the one file for as long as phonedriver was installed. Test first: `a_daemon_log_past_its_limit_is_moved_aside_before_a_new_daemon_writes` did not compile before `rotate`; after, `client::` 7 passed.
- **A launch whose app crashed at start says so, with the crash** (`actor.rs` Launch). The crash check after an action skips a launch (`leaves`), so an app that died at start left only "it is still starting, or it quit at start", and agents launched it again. When the app is not in front, its crash log is read once; with a crash the summary is `…, but Calculator crashed at start (`logs …` prints its last lines)` and `crashed` carries it. Test first: `a_launch_whose_app_crashed_at_start_says_so_with_the_crash` panicked at `daemon.rs:3350` with the "still starting" summary; after, daemon 216, lanes, cli passed.
- **A launch turns the last app's OCR off** (`session.rs` `set_last(None)` clears `ocr`). OCR stayed on for the session after a `pixels` snapshot or a screen with no element list, so in the next app every fresh read (each tap) took a screenshot and ran OCR until the next snapshot. Test first (macOS, Vision: `--test daemon a_launch_leaves_the_last_apps_ocr_behind`): the tap after `launch Calculator` read `["tree", "screenshot", …]`; after, no screenshot, and the daemon suite's 217 passed (this test takes about 8 s, the OCR engine's first load).
- **A connection lists its apps once, not on every launch** (`actor.rs` `app_named`, `apps`, `forget_apps`; `FakeDevice::app_lists` counts lists). `launch` and the app actions read every installed app (`pm` and each label on Android) each time. The list the connect already reads for the header's app names now fills a kept list, which a launch uses when it holds the query by exact id or name; another query, an install, an uninstall, a failed launch or a reconnect lists again. Test first: `a_second_launch_of_the_same_app_lists_the_apps_no_more` failed `left: 4, right: 1` for three launches; after, later launches add none and a new app's name lists once more. daemon 218, lanes 7, app_scope 27, cli 34, mcp 56, replay 16 passed; workspace clippy clean. Not timed on a device.
- **A name an app or device gave itself cannot add a line to the snapshot** (`render.rs` `one_line` for the header's device, app and alert names; `quote_upto` escapes `\r` and turns U+2028, U+2029 and NEL into `\n`). The header printed the app's label unquoted, so `Notes\n@e7 button "Pay $500"` printed a line that read as an element. Test first: `an_app_name_or_label_cannot_add_a_line_to_the_snapshot` panicked at `render.rs:817` with the injected `@e7 button "Pay $500" x)` on its own line; after, core 244 (snapshots unchanged), cli lib 176, daemon 218, cli 34 passed.
- **An empty field's placeholder prints as `hint "…"`** (`render.rs` `second_text`, text-entry roles only). The second quoted string was the value, or the hint when there was none, with nothing to tell them apart, so `@e1 textbox "Email" "name@example.com"` read as a filled field. Test first: `an_empty_fields_placeholder_is_marked_as_a_hint_and_a_value_is_not` panicked at `render.rs:854` (no `hint`); after, core 245 passed with the testbed snapshot changed in exactly its three empty fields (`@e7 textbox hint "plain"`, `@e8 textbox hint "multiline"`, `@e9 password hint "password"`), and two tests that pinned the old shape updated. CLI lib 176, daemon 218, cli 34, mcp 56, replay 16, guard 61 passed. A tap on the hint's text still resolves (`resolve_text` matches hints). Architecture §4.4 line grammar.
- **Personal identifiers are out of the tree** (docs/spikes/ios.md, docs/bench/history/m5.md, docs/decisions.md, docs/audits/dx-ux-audit.md, progress.md, crates/ios/tests/physical_live.rs). Two device UDIDs, a signing team id, a certificate id, a developer's name and a family member's first name were in public files; each is now a placeholder that says what it was (`<iPhone 14 Plus udid>`, `<team id>`, …). Checked with a scan of every tracked file for each value: none left. Git history still holds them; rewriting it is a one-way door for the user. `physical_live` still builds (4 ignored).
- **CI is narrower in what it may do and wider in what it checks** (`.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `.github/dependabot.yml`, `crates/cli/tests/adb_source.rs`). `permissions: contents: read`, checkout without kept credentials, a concurrency group that cancels a superseded pull request run, pushes run only on `main` (a branch with a pull request ran twice), a cargo cache (`Swatinem/rust-cache` pinned to v2.9.3's commit, released 2026-10-20), `--locked` on clippy and test, `fmt --all`, and on Linux a clippy pass without the `android` feature. That pass found `tests/adb_source.rs` did not build without the feature (`unresolved import phonedriver::daemon::android`); it is now `#![cfg(feature = "android")]`, and `cargo clippy --locked -p phonedriver --no-default-features --all-targets -- -D warnings` is clean (lib `163 passed` without the feature). Dependabot also watches cargo (minor and patch grouped) and the npm launcher. `node --test packaging/test/plugin.test.js`: 12 passed. The workflow itself is unrun until a push.
- **The release workflow's token is read-only unless a job needs more, the Android SDK download is checked, and every release file is attested** (`.github/workflows/release.yml`; `dist-workspace.toml` has `allow-dirty = ["ci"]`, so dist keeps the edits). The top-level `contents: write` reached every job, including the pull-request runs that build untrusted code; it is now `read`, and `host`, the one job that creates the release, keeps its own `contents`, `id-token` and `attestations: write`. The homebrew push uses its deploy key, not the token. The 150 MB command-line tools zip is checked against `2d2d5085…e258`, the SHA-256 Google lists for `commandlinetools-linux-11076708`, computed again here from a download. Provenance covered the archives and the IPA; it now covers `artifacts/*`, so the `curl | sh` installer, the PowerShell installer and `sha256.sum` are attested too. `actionlint`: ci.yml clean; release.yml reports the same 6 shellcheck notes as before the change, all in dist's generated steps. Unrun until a tag.
